# Chapter 9-g — Group G: C-PAIR

**Document:** `NH_MASTER-21_SYSTEM_BEHAVIOR_v0_1_CANDIDATE__CH09-g.md`  
**Status:** CANDIDATE  
**Source repository:** `nesgeva/NH-GOVERNANCE`  
**Source commit:** `6a7160ba688ba4e433a31899162815df7e2bab17`  

This piece covers V10 §§25.7–25.10 in full: initial owner-phone pairing, normal recovery-code creation and handover, replacement, atomic emergency recovery, and pairing-owned facts consumed by enrollment. The canonical biometric artifacts and maintenance factors remain in CH09-e and CH09-f. Full enrollment coordination belongs to CH09-h, modes to CH09-i, durable-operation mechanics to CH10-b, side paths to CH11 and final registers to CH12. No unstated code format, emergency verification algorithm, QR regeneration route or crash transaction mechanism is selected.

[SOURCE CONFLICT] Companion embedded security-design §§7, 8 and 10 describe destroying QR/temporary secrets, destroying failed new recovery codes, erasing the activated phone-local copy and destroying provisional emergency material. V10 §§25.7–25.10 instead requires permanent inertness, sealing or inaccessibility at the stated point. V10's rules are retained; the older Companion text is not applied. Literal state and event names containing `destroyed` retain V10's explicitly non-destructive semantics. [COMP §7. Initial Owner-Phone Pairing] [COMP §8. Recovery-Code Lifecycle] [COMP §10. Atomic Emergency Recovery Flow] [V10 §25.7] [V10 §25.8] [V10 §25.10]

Citation keys: V10 = `01_AUTHORITATIVE/NH_MASTER-20_CORRECTED_v10.md`; MAP = `02_WORKING_MAP/NH_COMPLETE_DESIGN_AND_WIRING_MAP_v1_6_CANDIDATE.md`; COMP = `01_AUTHORITATIVE/NH_PROJECT_COMPANION_GOVERNANCE_AND_ARCHIVE_v1.md`; `04/` = `04_ACCEPTED_STANDALONE_DESIGNS/`; `05/` = `05_ACTIVE_CANDIDATE/`.

### C-PAIR — Owner-phone pairing, recovery, replacement, emergency (§25.7–25.10)
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §25.7] [V10 §25.8] [V10 §25.9] [V10 §25.10] [MAP C-PAIR]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The owner of phone trust, initial setup, normal recovery-code handover, replacement and atomic emergency recovery. [MAP C-PAIR]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — The first phone's hardware-backed identity; the normal recovery-code save/check/test results; the current code plus motherbase thumbprint for replacement; or all simultaneous emergency factors and all three new-material verification results. [V10 §25.7] [V10 §25.8] [V10 §25.9] [V10 §25.10]
- Does: DESIGNED — Progresses through the four exact initial states, permanently closes the used QR path, activates a verified and locally tested normal code before invalidating the old one, replaces a fully paired/verified phone with automatic prior-phone revocation, and commits emergency trust/material changes only as one complete atomic change. [V10 §25.7] [V10 §25.8] [V10 §25.9] [V10 §25.10]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — Current trusted-phone, recovery, QR and setup facts; the stated security audit events; and either the successful trust/material handover or the source-defined safe retained prior state. [MAP C-PAIR]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Reopen a used initial QR, confer permanent trust at a provisional stage, expose a normal recovery code on the desktop or clipboard, activate it before the local test succeeds, perform remote emergency recovery, or partially revoke old emergency trust/material. [V10 §25.7] [V10 §25.8] [V10 §25.10]
- Fails closed by: DESIGNED — Keeps failed/timed-out initial recovery setup on the same provisional phone; leaves the old code valid on unconfirmed timeout or three save-check failures; aborts any failed precommit emergency step with new provisional material sealed/inert, provisional pairing cancelled and all prior valid trust/material preserved. [V10 §25.7 / Four States] [V10 §25.8] [V10 §25.10 / If Any Step Fails Before Final Commit]

[V10 §25.6] [V10 §25.13 / Purpose-Specific Authorization]

### C-PAIR.1 — Initial pairing lifecycle
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §25.7 / Four States]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The four-state initial owner-phone pairing lifecycle. [V10 §25.7 / Four States]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — A signed QR, the first successful phone pairing and the first recovery-code handover results. [V10 §25.7 / Four States]
- Does: DESIGNED — Progresses through `qr_available`, `phone_provisionally_paired_qr_destroyed`, `recovery_setup_in_progress`, and `owner_setup_finalized_qr_path_permanently_closed` under their stated conditions. The legacy `destroyed` name means permanently inert and sealed. [V10 §25.7 / Four States]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — Provisional phone trust after successful pairing, then permanent owner trust and permanent QR closure only after all first-code steps succeed. [V10 §25.7 / Four States]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Treat successful scanning alone as finalized owner setup or regenerate the QR after state 2. [V10 §25.7 / Four States]
- Fails closed by: DESIGNED — A recovery-setup failure or timeout leaves state 3 and provisional pairing intact for a same-phone retry. [V10 §25.7 / Four States]

TOGETHER
- Fed by: DESIGNED — C-PAIR.1.1 — QR available: supplies the initial state; C-PAIR.1.2 — Phone provisionally paired: supplies the successful pairing transition; C-PAIR.1.3 — Recovery setup in progress: supplies the incomplete setup state; C-PAIR.1.4 — Owner setup finalized: supplies the complete handover transition. [V10 §25.7 / Four States]
- Gated by: DESIGNED — C-PAIR.1.4 — Owner setup finalized: final permanent owner status is permitted only after its four first-code conditions succeed. [V10 §25.7 / Four States]
- Changes: NOT DECIDED

Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_B_INT_7_INITIAL_NESS_VOICE_PROFILE_ENROLLMENT_BOOTSTRAP_WIRING_v1_1_CANDIDATE.md §9]

ALONE

- Must never: ACCEPTED — Permit silent resumption of the same capture session or reconstruction of missing audio. [04/NH_B_INT_7_INITIAL_NESS_VOICE_PROFILE_ENROLLMENT_BOOTSTRAP_WIRING_v1_1_CANDIDATE.md §9]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — A later enrollment attempt needs an explicit begin, fresh prerequisite check, fresh token and new session identity. [04/NH_B_INT_7_INITIAL_NESS_VOICE_PROFILE_ENROLLMENT_BOOTSTRAP_WIRING_v1_1_CANDIDATE.md §9]
